中文视频提示词：
当代中国大学宿舍，雨夜。写实胶片质感，2.39:1宽银幕，35mm颗粒，冷蓝窗光衬托暖黄台灯，现代生活质感；画面内不出现军装、战壕与硝烟。 大远景，缓慢横移。深夜的宿舍只亮着一盏台灯。桌上散着旧太原地图、摊开的笔记、翻卷的战争小说和一册布面回忆录，所有封面与日期都留白；窗外大雨沿玻璃蜿蜒而下。

English video prompt：
Contemporary Chinese university dormitory on a rainy night. Realistic film look, 35mm grain, 2.39:1 widescreen, cold blue window light against a warm yellow desk lamp. No military uniforms, trenches or gunsmoke in frame. Extreme wide shot, slow lateral dolly. A late-night dormitory lit only by one desk lamp. An old Taiyuan city map, a notebook, a dog-eared war novel and a clothbound memoir are spread across the desk, every cover and date left blank paper for post-production titles. Heavy rain streams down the windowpane.

中文视频提示词：
1945年8月上旬，架空历史支线里的华北太原战场（非真实史实）。写实战争电影，35mm胶片颗粒，低饱和青灰与土黄，烟尘弥漫、自然硬光、轻微手持呼吸感，2.39:1宽银幕。八路军穿褪色灰布军装、绑腿与布鞋；日军穿沾满尘土的土黄偏橄榄绿军服。布景、器物与武器符合20世纪40年代质感，人物外貌与前后镜头保持统一。 大远景，手持。太原南门外：灰褐城墙伏在硝烟中，碉堡群像一排沉默的兽脊，天空压得很低，焦土上散着铁丝网残骸。

English video prompt：
Early August 1945, alternate-history battlefield outside Taiyuan, north China (fiction, not real events). Realistic WWII war film, 35mm film grain, desaturated slate-blue and earth-yellow palette, drifting smoke and dust, natural hard light, subtle handheld breathing, 2.39:1 anamorphic widescreen. Eighth Route Army troops in faded grey cotton uniforms, cloth leg wraps and cloth shoes; Japanese troops in dust-caked khaki-olive uniforms. Period-accurate 1940s props, weapons and set dressing; identical character appearance across shots. Extreme wide shot, handheld. August 1945, alternate timeline: beyond the south gate of Taiyuan, grey-brown city walls crouch in gunsmoke and a chain of blockhouses sits along the plain like the silent spines of beasts.

首帧图中文提示词：
1945年华北战场，写实战争电影感，低饱和青灰与土黄，烟尘弥漫，自然硬光，35mm颗粒。大远景，手持。太原南门外：灰褐城墙伏在硝烟中，碉堡群像一排沉默的兽脊，天空压得很低，焦土上散着铁丝网残骸。 无任何可读文字，电影感静帧，2.39:1宽银幕。

Cast: CHEN XIAOMAN / CHEN FENG (1945: same face, thinner and sun-darkened, short stiff buzz cut, faded grey cotton uniform, cloth leg wraps, cloth shoes, bandaged right hand, faded red rag at the wrist).

1945年8月上旬，架空历史支线里的华北太原战场（非真实史实）。写实战争电影，35mm胶片颗粒，低饱和青灰与土黄，烟尘弥漫、自然硬光、轻微手持呼吸感，2.39:1宽银幕。八路军穿褪色灰布军装、绑腿与布鞋；日军穿沾满尘土的土黄偏橄榄绿军服。布景、器物与武器符合20世纪40年代质感，人物外貌与前后镜头保持统一。 中景，低机位。坑道尽头，工兵将标记过的木箱送入指定位置。陈峰借罩灯的一缕微光辨认旧墙脚上的修补痕迹，满面尘土。 人物：陈小满／陈峰（同脸·瘦黑·短硬寸头·灰布军装·绑腿·布鞋·右手包扎·腕系褪色红布）。

English video prompt：
Early August 1945, alternate-history battlefield outside Taiyuan, north China (fiction, not real events). Realistic WWII war film, 35mm film grain, desaturated slate-blue and earth-yellow palette, drifting smoke and dust, natural hard light, subtle handheld breathing, 2.39:1 anamorphic widescreen. Eighth Route Army troops in faded grey cotton uniforms, cloth leg wraps and cloth shoes; Japanese troops in dust-caked khaki-olive uniforms. Period-accurate 1940s props, weapons and set dressing; identical character appearance across shots. Medium shot, low camera. Medium, low camera: at the end of the tunnel sappers slide marked crates into position; he tilts a shaded lamp to read the repair marks on the old wall footing, his face caked in dust. Cast: CHEN XIAOMAN / CHEN FENG (1945: same face, thinner and sun-darkened, short stiff buzz cut, faded grey cotton uniform, cloth leg wraps, cloth shoes, bandaged right hand, faded red rag at the wrist).

1945年8月上旬，架空历史支线里的华北太原战场（非真实史实）。写实战争电影，35mm胶片颗粒，低饱和青灰与土黄，烟尘弥漫、自然硬光、轻微手持呼吸感，2.39:1宽银幕。八路军穿褪色灰布军装、绑腿与布鞋；日军穿沾满尘土的土黄偏橄榄绿军服。布景、器物与武器符合20世纪40年代质感，人物外貌与前后镜头保持统一。 全景，固定。灰砖洋楼院里，守军在门前设了临时障碍，几名伤兵缩在墙下。山田将文件扔进火盆，抬头却看见另一侧墙外也有中国军队逼近。 人物：山田健二（矮壮·鬓角花白·八字胡·军装笔挺·白手套·军刀·皮靴）。

English video prompt：
Early August 1945, alternate-history battlefield outside Taiyuan, north China (fiction, not real events). Realistic WWII war film, 35mm film grain, desaturated slate-blue and earth-yellow palette, drifting smoke and dust, natural hard light, subtle handheld breathing, 2.39:1 anamorphic widescreen. Eighth Route Army troops in faded grey cotton uniforms, cloth leg wraps and cloth shoes; Japanese troops in dust-caked khaki-olive uniforms. Period-accurate 1940s props, weapons and set dressing; identical character appearance across shots. Wide shot, locked-off static camera. Wide static: in the courtyard of a grey-brick colonial building the garrison has thrown up a makeshift barricade, wounded men huddled under the wall; a Japanese colonel feeds documents into a brazier, then looks up at Chinese troops closing in from the other side. Cast: COLONEL YAMADA KENJI (short and thick-set, greying temples, toothbrush moustache, immaculate uniform, white gloves, officer's sword, riding boots).
